Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! This week, we’re looking at a 64-player [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20221007121403/https://magic.wizards.com/en/articles/archive/2009-06-29) *Dark Ascension*-*Innistrad* draft.

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/da948_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20120411000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=3697738)

### Champion

**AgraveiN, 2-0**
